Governs the comprehensive Experimentation and\n  4|Benchmarking Campaign that begins when all roadmap phases are complete\n  5|(Development Workflow Standards §3, \"Campaign trigger\"), and any ad-hoc\n  6|scientific experiment run before then. Complements — and never overrides — the\n  7|[Benchmarking and Reproducibility Standards](Benchmarking_and_Reproducibility_Standards.md).\n  8|\n  9|---\n 10|\n 11|## 1. Principles\n 12|\n 13|1. **Pre-registration is mandatory.** No experiment executes before its\n 14|   pre-registration document — including expected results and failure\n 15|   conditions — is committed. Post-hoc hypotheses are labeled exploratory and\n 16|   can never be reported as confirmatory.\n 17|2. **Failure conditions are declared up front.** Every experiment states, in\n 18|   advance, what outcome would falsify the hypothesis, what constitutes an\n 19|   invalid run (abort criteria), and what will be reported if the experiment\n 20|   fails. Negative results are reported with the same rigor as positive ones.\n 21|3. **One experiment, one immutable record.** Pre-registration documents are\n 22|   frozen at execution time; deviations from protocol are recorded in the\n 23|   report as protocol deviations, never edited into the pre-registration.\n 24|4. **Reproducibility is a precondition, not an afterthought.** An experiment\n 25|   that cannot be re-run from its artefacts (seeds, config, environment\n 26|   capture, code SHA) is invalid regardless of its results.\n 27|5. **Claims match evidence.** Every scientific claim in a report traces to a\n 28|   stored artefact; effect sizes and uncertainty are always reported alongside\n 29|   significance.\n 30|\n 31|## 2. Experiment lifecycle\n 32|\n 33|```\n 34|E1 Pre-registration ──► E2 Review/approval ──► E3 Execution ──► E4 Analysis ──► E5 Report\n 35|        (frozen)            (maintainer)          (logged)        (per plan)      (archived)\n 36|```\n 37|\n 38|Validation-run supervision at every stage follows Testing Standards §7\n 39|(Project Plan §8.3, amendment #49): no arbitrary whole-run wall-clock kill while a run is\n 40|demonstrably progressing. Pre-registered resource budgets, abort criteria,\n 41|and statistical parameters (E1 items 5 and 10) are unchanged by it.\n 42|\n 43|### E1 — Pre-registration\n 44|\n 45|Create `DOCS/experiments/EXP-NNN-<slug>/preregistration.md` from\n 46|`DOCS/experiments/TEMPLATE_Preregistration.md`. Required sections:\n 47|\n 48|1. **Identification** — EXP ID, title, authors, date, code version (git SHA +\n 49|   `prin` version), related plan requirement(s).\n 50|2. **Background and motivation** — what question this answers; citations.\n 51|3. **Hypotheses** — numbered `H1..Hn`, each falsifiable and quantitative\n 52|   (e.g. \"H1: PhaseTracker IP ≥ SlotAttention IP + 0.05 on temporal CLEVR-6\n 53|   at matched parameter budget\").\n 54|4. **Expected results** — per hypothesis: predicted direction, predicted\n 55|   magnitude (point estimate or range), and the basis for the prediction\n 56|   (PRINet 3.0 results, theory, pilot data).\n 57|5. **Failure conditions** — per hypothesis: the outcome that falsifies it;\n 58|   plus experiment-level **abort criteria** (e.g. NaN guard trips, order\n 59|   parameter leaves [0,1], run-time budget exceeded, seed irreproducibility)\n 60|   that render a run invalid rather than negative.\n 61|6. **Method** — exact configurations: model variants, datasets, N/K/dt\n 62|   parameter grids, integrator, backend, hardware, dtype.\n 63|7. **Variables and controls** — independent/dependent variables, confounders\n 64|   controlled (matched loss/optimizer/augmentation/parameter budgets per the\n 65|   fair-training framework), baselines.\n 66|8. **Sample plan and statistics** — number of seeds (≥10 for confirmatory\n 67|   claims unless justified), statistical tests (Welch t-test, bootstrap CIs\n 68|   at 95%), effect-size measure (Cohen's d or Cliff's delta), multiple-\n 69|   comparison correction (Holm–Bonferroni when >2 comparisons), and the\n 70|   significance threshold (α = 0.05 unless justified).\n 71|9. **Analysis plan** — exactly which artefacts, figures, and tables will be\n 72|   produced and how each hypothesis will be adjudicated from them.\n 73|10. **Resource budget** — expected GPU/CPU hours and storage.\n 74|\n 75|### E2 — Review and approval\n 76|\n 77|- The maintainer reviews for: falsifiability, statistical adequacy, fair\n 78|  baselines, and resource sanity.
